</tr><tr><td class="sidebar-navbar" style="border-top:1px solid #aaa;"><span class="mw-empty-elt"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1333133064" about="#mwt23" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Hlist/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1239400231" about="#mwt24" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Navbar/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div class="navbar plainlinks hlist navbar-mini"><ul><li class="nv-view"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:HTML" title="Template:HTML"><abbr title="View this template">v</abbr></a></li><li class="nv-talk"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template_talk:HTML" title="Template talk:HTML"><abbr title="Discuss this template">t</abbr></a></li><li class="nv-edit"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:EditPage/Template:HTML" title="Special:EditPage/Template:HTML"><abbr title="Edit this template">e</abbr></a></li></ul></div></td></tr></tbody></table>

<p id="mwDw"><b id="mwEA">Cascading Style Sheets</b> (<b id="mwEQ">CSS</b>) is a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Style_sheet_language" title="Style sheet language" id="mwEg">style sheet language</a> used for specifying the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presentation_semantics" title="Presentation semantics" id="mwEw">presentation</a> and styling of a document written in a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Markup_language" title="Markup language" id="mwFA">markup language</a>, such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML" title="HTML" id="mwFQ">HTML</a> or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XML" title="XML" id="mwFg">XML</a> (including XML dialects such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SVG" title="SVG" id="mwFw">SVG</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MathML" title="MathML" id="mwGA">MathML</a>, or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XHTML" title="XHTML" id="mwGQ">XHTML</a>).<sup about="#mwt27" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-2&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-2" id="mwGg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHA">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHQ">]</span></span></a></sup> CSS is a cornerstone technology of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Wide_Web" title="World Wide Web" id="mwHg">World Wide Web</a>, alongside HTML and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/JavaScript" title="JavaScript" id="mwHw">JavaScript</a>.<sup about="#mwt30" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-3&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-3" id="mwIA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIg">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwJA">CSS is designed to enable the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Separation_of_content_and_presentation" title="Separation of content and presentation" id="mwJQ">separation of content and presentation</a>, including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Page_layout" title="Page layout" id="mwJg">layout</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Color" title="Color" id="mwJw">colors</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Typeface" title="Typeface" id="mwKA">fonts</a>.<sup about="#mwt33" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-4&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-4" id="mwKQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwKg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLA">]</span></span></a></sup> This separation can improve content <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Accessibility" title="Accessibility" id="mwLQ">accessibility</a>, since the content can be written without concern for its presentation; provide more flexibility and control in the specification of presentation characteristics; enable multiple <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_page" title="Web page" id="mwLg">web pages</a> to share formatting by specifying the relevant CSS in a separate .css file, which reduces complexity and repetition in the structural content; and enable the .css file to be <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cache_(computing)" title="Cache (computing)" id="mwLw">cached</a> to improve the page load speed between the pages that share the file and its formatting.</p>

<p id="mwMA">Separation of formatting and content also makes it feasible to present the same markup page in different styles for different rendering methods, such as on-screen, in print, by voice (via speech-based browser or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Screen_reader" title="Screen reader" id="mwMQ">screen reader</a>), and on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Braille_display" title="Braille display" class="mw-redirect" id="mwMg">Braille-based</a> tactile devices. CSS also has rules for alternative formatting if the content is accessed on a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mobile_device" title="Mobile device" id="mwMw">mobile device</a>.<sup about="#mwt36" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-5&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwNA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNg">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwOA">The name <i id="mwOQ">cascading</i> comes from the specified priority scheme to determine which declaration applies if more than one declaration of a property match a particular element. This cascading priority scheme is predictable.</p>

<p id="mwOg">The CSS specifications are maintained by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Wide_Web_Consortium" title="World Wide Web Consortium" id="mwOw">World Wide Web Consortium</a> (W3C). Internet media type (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MIME_media_type" title="MIME media type" class="mw-redirect" id="mwPA">MIME type</a>) <code id="mwPQ">text/css</code> is registered for use with CSS by RFC 2318 (March 1998). The W3C operates a free <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/W3C_Markup_Validation_Service#CSS_validation" title="W3C Markup Validation Service" id="mwPg">CSS validation service</a> for CSS documents.<sup about="#mwt39" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-6&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwPw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQQ">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwQw">In addition to HTML, other markup languages support the use of CSS including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XHTML" title="XHTML" id="mwRA">XHTML</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plain_Old_XML" title="Plain Old XML" id="mwRQ">plain XML</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SVG" title="SVG" id="mwRg">SVG</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XUL" title="XUL" id="mwRw">XUL</a>. CSS is also used in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GTK" title="GTK" id="mwSA">GTK</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Widget_toolkit" title="Widget toolkit" id="mwSQ">widget toolkit</a>.</p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwSg" data-mw="{&quot;autoGenerated&quot;:true}"></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwSw" aria-labelledby="Syntax"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Syntax">Syntax</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Syntax"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwTA">CSS has a simple <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Syntax_(programming)" title="Syntax (programming)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwTQ">syntax</a> and uses a number of English keywords to specify the names of various style properties.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwTg" aria-labelledby="Style_sheet"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Style_sheet">Style sheet</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Style sheet"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt40" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwTw" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Style sheet (web development)&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt41" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt40" id="mwUA">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Style_sheet_(web_development)" title="Style sheet (web development)">Style sheet (web development)</a></div>
<p id="mwUQ">A style sheet consists of a list of <i id="mwUg">rules</i>. Each rule or rule-set consists of one or more <i id="mwUw"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#Selector" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwVA">selectors</a></i>, and a <i id="mwVQ"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="#Declaration_block" class="mw-selflink-fragment" id="mwVg">declaration block</a></i>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwVw" aria-labelledby="Selector"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Selector">Selector</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Selector"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt42" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwWA" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Redirect&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Redirect&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;CSS class&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;element classes that are part of the HTML content model upon which the CSS class selector syntax depends&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;class attribute (HTML)&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt43" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt42" id="mwWQ">"CSS class" redirects here. For element classes that are part of the HTML content model upon which the CSS class selector syntax depends, see <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Class_attribute_(HTML)" title="Class attribute (HTML)" class="mw-redirect">class attribute (HTML)</a>.</div>

<p id="mwWg">In CSS, <i id="mwWw">selectors</i> declare which part of the markup a style applies to by matching tags and attributes in the markup itself.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwXA" aria-labelledby="Selector_types"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Selector_types">Selector types</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Selector types"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwXQ">Selectors may apply to the following:</p>
<ul id="mwXg"><li id="mwXw">all <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML_element" title="HTML element" id="mwYA">elements</a> of a specific type, e.g. the second-level headers <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML_element#Basic_text" title="HTML element" id="mwYQ">h2</a></li>
<li id="mwYg">elements specified by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML_attribute" title="HTML attribute" id="mwYw">attribute</a>, in particular:
<ul id="mwZA"><li id="mwZQ"><i id="mwZg">id</i>: an identifier unique within the document, denoted in the selector language by a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Number_sign" title="Number sign" id="mwZw">hash</a> prefix e.g. <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-text mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt44" id="mwaA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;text&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;#id&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;#id&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">#id</code></li>
<li id="mwaQ"><i id="mwag">class</i>: an identifier that can annotate multiple elements in a document, denoted by a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Period_(punctuation)" title="Period (punctuation)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwaw">dot</a> prefix e.g. <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-text mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt46" id="mwbA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;text&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;.classname&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;.classname&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">.classname</code> (the phrase "CSS class", although sometimes used, is a misnomer, as element classes—specified with the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Class_attribute_(HTML)" title="Class attribute (HTML)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwbQ">HTML class attribute</a>—is a markup feature that is distinct from browsers' CSS subsystems, and the related W3C/WHATWG standards work on document styles; see <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Resource_Description_Framework" title="Resource Description Framework" id="mwbg">RDF</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microformat" title="Microformat" id="mwbw">microformats</a> for the origins of the "class" system of the Web content model)</li></ul></li>
<li id="mwcA">elements depending on how they are placed relative to others in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Document_Object_Model" title="Document Object Model" id="mwcQ">document tree</a>.</li></ul>

<p id="mwcg">Classes and IDs are case-sensitive, start with letters, and can include alphanumeric characters, hyphens, and underscores. A class may apply to any number of instances of any element. An ID may only be applied to a single element.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwcw" aria-labelledby="Pseudo-classes"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Pseudo-classes">Pseudo-classes</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Pseudo-classes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt48" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwdA" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;redirect&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Redirect&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Pseudo-element&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;pseudoelement symbols in chemistry&quot;},&quot;3&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Skeletal formula#Pseudoelement symbols&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt49" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt48" id="mwdQ">"Pseudo-element" redirects here. For pseudoelement symbols in chemistry, see <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skeletal_formula#Pseudoelement_symbols" title="Skeletal formula">Skeletal formula §<span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span>Pseudoelement symbols</a>.</div>

<p id="mwdg"><i id="mwdw">Pseudo-classes</i> are used in CSS selectors to permit formatting based on information that is not contained in the document tree.</p>

<p id="mweA">One example of a widely used pseudo-class is <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt50" id="mweQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;:hover&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;:hover&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mweg">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwew">hover</span></code>, which identifies content only when the user "points to" the visible element, usually by holding the mouse cursor over it. It is appended to a selector as in <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt52" id="mwfA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;a:hover&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;a:hover&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwfQ">a</span><span class="p" id="mwfg">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwfw">hover</span></code> or <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt54" id="mwgA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;#elementid:hover&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;#elementid:hover&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mwgQ">#</span><span class="nn" id="mwgg">elementid</span><span class="p" id="mwgw">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwhA">hover</span></code>.</p>

<p id="mwhQ">A pseudo-class classifies document elements, such as <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt56" id="mwhg" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;:link&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;:link&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mwhw">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwiA">link</span></code> or <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt58" id="mwiQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;:visited&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;:visited&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mwig">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwiw">visited</span></code>, whereas a <i id="mwjA">pseudo-element</i> makes a selection that may consist of partial elements, such as <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt60" id="mwjQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;::first-line&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;::first-line&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mwjg">::</span><span class="nd" id="mwjw">first-line</span></code> or <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt62" id="mwkA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;::first-letter&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;::first-letter&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mwkQ">::</span><span class="nd" id="mwkg">first-letter</span></code>.<sup about="#mwt66" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-7&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwkw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwlA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlQ">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlg">]</span></span></a></sup> Note the distinction between the double-colon notation used for pseudo-elements and the single-colon notation used for pseudo-classes.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mwlw" aria-labelledby="Combinators"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Combinators">Combinators</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Combinators"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwmA">Multiple simple selectors may be joined using combinators to specify elements by location, element type, id, class, or any combination thereof.<sup about="#mwt69" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-8&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mwmQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwmg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwmw">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnA">]</span></span></a></sup> The order of the selectors is important. For example, <code class="nowrap mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt70" id="mwnQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;nowrap&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\ndiv .myClass {color: red;}&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwng">div</span><span class="w" id="mwnw"> </span><span class="p" id="mwoA">.</span><span class="nc" id="mwoQ">myClass</span><span class="w" id="mwog"> </span><span class="p" id="mwow">{</span><span class="k" id="mwpA">color</span><span class="p" id="mwpQ">:</span><span class="w" id="mwpg"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwpw">red</span><span class="p" id="mwqA">;}</span></code> applies to all elements of class myClass that are inside div elements, whereas <code class="nowrap mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt71" id="mwqQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;nowrap&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;.myClass div {color: red;}&quot;}}"><span class="p" id="mwqg">.</span><span class="nc" id="mwqw">myClass</span><span class="w" id="mwrA"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwrQ">div</span><span class="w" id="mwrg"> </span><span class="p" id="mwrw">{</span><span class="k" id="mwsA">color</span><span class="p" id="mwsQ">:</span><span class="w" id="mwsg"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwsw">red</span><span class="p" id="mwtA">;}</span></code> applies to all div elements that are inside elements of class myClass. This is not to be confused with concatenated identifiers such as <code class="nowrap mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt72" id="mwtQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;nowrap&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\ndiv.myClass {color: red;}&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwtg">div</span><span class="p" id="mwtw">.</span><span class="nc" id="mwuA">myClass</span><span class="w" id="mwuQ"> </span><span class="p" id="mwug">{</span><span class="k" id="mwuw">color</span><span class="p" id="mwvA">:</span><span class="w" id="mwvQ"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwvg">red</span><span class="p" id="mwvw">;}</span></code> which applies to div elements of class myClass.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwwA" aria-labelledby="Summary_of_selector_syntax"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Summary_of_selector_syntax">Summary of selector syntax</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Summary of selector syntax"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwwQ">The following table provides a summary of selector syntax indicating usage and the version of CSS that introduced it.<sup about="#mwt75" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-9&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwwg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwww"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxA">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<table class="wikitable" id="mwxg">
<caption id="mwxw">CSS selectors</caption>
<tbody id="mwyA"><tr id="mwyQ">
<th id="mwyg">Pattern</th><th id="mwyw">Matches</th><th id="mwzA">First defined<br id="mwzQ">in CSS level</th></tr>
<tr id="mwzg">
<td id="mwzw"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt76" id="mw0A" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mw0Q">E</span></code></td><td id="mw0g">an element of type E</td><td id="mw0w">1</td></tr>
<tr id="mw1A">
<td id="mw1Q"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt78" id="mw1g" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E:link&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E:link&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mw1w">E</span><span class="p" id="mw2A">:</span><span class="nd" id="mw2Q">link</span></code></td><td rowspan="2" id="mw2g">an E element that is the source anchor of a hyperlink whose target is either not yet visited (:link) or already visited (:visited)</td><td rowspan="2" id="mw2w">1</td></tr>
<tr id="mw3A">
<td id="mw3Q"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt80" id="mw3g" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E:visited&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E:visited&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mw3w">E</span><span class="p" id="mw4A">:</span><span class="nd" id="mw4Q">visited</span></code></td></tr>
<tr id="mw4g">

<td id="mwAi8"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt160" id="mwAjA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E:enabled&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E:enabled&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAjE">E</span><span class="p" id="mwAjI">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwAjM">enabled</span></code></td><td id="mwAjQ">a user interface element E that is enabled</td><td id="mwAjU">3</td></tr>
<tr id="mwAjY">
<td id="mwAjc"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt162" id="mwAjg" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E:disabled&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E:disabled&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAjk">E</span><span class="p" id="mwAjo">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwAjs">disabled</span></code></td><td id="mwAjw">a user interface element E that is disabled</td><td id="mwAj0">3</td></tr>
<tr id="mwAj4">
<td id="mwAj8"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt164" id="mwAkA" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E:checked&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E:checked&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAkE">E</span><span class="p" id="mwAkI">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwAkM">checked</span></code></td><td id="mwAkQ">a user interface element E that is checked (for instance a radio button or checkbox)</td><td id="mwAkU">3</td></tr>
<tr id="mwAkY">
<td id="mwAkc"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt166" id="mwAkg" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E:not(s)&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E:not(s)&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAkk">E</span><span class="p" id="mwAko">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwAks">not</span><span class="o" id="mwAkw">(</span><span class="nt" id="mwAk0">s</span><span class="o" id="mwAk4">)</span></code></td><td id="mwAk8">an E element that does not match simple selector s</td><td id="mwAlA">3</td></tr>
<tr id="mwAlE">
<td id="mwAlI"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt168" id="mwAlM" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;E ~ F&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;E ~ F&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAlQ">E</span><span class="w" id="mwAlU"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAlY">~</span><span class="w" id="mwAlc"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwAlg">F</span></code></td><td id="mwAlk">an F element preceded by an E element</td><td id="mwAlo">3</td></tr>
<tr id="mwAls">

</tbody></table>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwAmY" aria-labelledby="Declaration_block"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Declaration_block">Declaration block</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Declaration block"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAmc">A declaration block consists of a pair of braces (<code id="mwAmg">{}</code>) enclosing a semicolon-separated list of <i id="mwAmk">declarations</i>.<sup about="#mwt174" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-10&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwAmo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAms"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmw">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwAm4" aria-labelledby="Declaration"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Declaration">Declaration</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Declaration"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAm8">Each declaration itself consists of a <i id="mwAnA">property</i>, a colon (<code id="mwAnE">:</code>), and a <i id="mwAnI">value</i>. Optional white-space may be around the declaration block, declarations, colons, and semi-colons for readability.<sup about="#mwt177" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-11" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-11&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-11" id="mwAnM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAnQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnU">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwAnc" aria-labelledby="Properties"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Properties">Properties</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Properties"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAng">Properties are specified in the CSS standard. Each property has a set of possible values. Some properties can affect any type of element, and others apply only to particular groups of elements.<sup about="#mwt180" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-12" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-12&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-12" id="mwAnk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAno"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAns">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt183" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-13&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwAn0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAn4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAn8">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="11" id="mwAoE" aria-labelledby="Values"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Values">Values</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Values"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAoI">Values may be keywords, such as "center" or "inherit", or numerical values, such as <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt184" id="mwAoM" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;200px&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;200px&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAoQ">200px</span></code> (200 pixels), <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt186" id="mwAoU" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;50vw&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;50vw&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwAoY">50vw</span></code> (50 percent of the viewport width) or <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r886049734" about="#mwt188" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwAoc" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Mono/styles.css&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;mono&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Mono&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;80%&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="monospaced" about="#mwt188" id="mwAog">80%</span> (80 percent of the parent element's width).</p>

<p id="mwAok">Color values can be specified with keywords (e.g. "<code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight class="nt" id="mwAos">red</span></code>"), hexadecimal values (e.g. <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt192" id="mwAow" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;#FF0000&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;#FF0000&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="p" id="mwAo0">#</span><span class="nn" id="mwAo4">FF0000</span></code>, also abbreviated as <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" class="o" id="mwApc">,</span><span class="w" id="mwApg"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwApk">0</span><span class="o" id="mwApo">,</span><span class="w" id="mwAps"> </span><span class="nt" 100% 50% / 0.8)&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwArk">hsl</span><span class="o" id="mwAro">(</span><span class="nt" id="mwArs">0</span><span class="w" id="mwArw"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwAr0">100</span><span class="o" id="mwAr4">%</span><span class="w" id="mwAr8"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwAsA">50</span><span class="o" id="mwAsE">%</span><span class="w" id="mwAsI"> </span><span class="o" id="mwAsM">/</span><span class="w" id="mwAsQ"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwAsU">0</span><span class="p" id="mwAsY">.</span><span class="nc" id="mwAsc">8</span><span class="o" id="mwAsg">)</span></code>).<sup about="#mwt206" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-14" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-14&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-14" id="mwAsk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAso"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAss">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAs0">Non-zero numeric values representing linear measures must include a length unit, which is either an alphabetic code or abbreviation, as in <code id="mwAs4">200px</code> or <code id="mwAs8">50vw</code>; or a percentage sign, as in <code id="mwAtA">80%</code>. Some units – <code id="mwAtE">cm</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Centimetre" title="Centimetre" id="mwAtI">centimetre</a>); <code id="mwAtM">in</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inch" title="Inch" id="mwAtQ">inch</a>); <code id="mwAtU">mm</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Millimetre" title="Millimetre" id="mwAtY">millimetre</a>); <code id="mwAtc">pc</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pica_(unit)" title="Pica (unit)" id="mwAtg">pica</a>); and <code id="mwAtk">pt</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Point_(typography)" title="Point (typography)" id="mwAto">point</a>) – are <i id="mwAts">absolute</i>, which means that the rendered dimension does not depend upon the structure of the page; others – <code id="mwAtw">em</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Em_(typography)" title="Em (typography)" id="mwAt0">em</a>); <code id="mwAt4">ex</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ex_(typography)" title="Ex (typography)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAt8">ex</a>) and <code id="mwAuA">px</code> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pixel" title="Pixel" id="mwAuE">pixel</a>)<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Wikipedia_articles_needing_clarification_from_March_2022" about="#mwt207" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAuI" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;clarify&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Clarify&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;reason&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;This \&quot;such as\&quot; is carrying too much weight. The size of a px is relative not to the font size, but to screen resolution and viewing distance. Furthermore, in CSS 3 the \&quot;px\&quot; becomes the primordial absolute unit as the length units become anchored to it.&quot;},&quot;date&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;March 2022&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><sup class="noprint Inline-Template " style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt207" id="mwAuM"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Please_clarify" title="Wikipedia:Please clarify"><span title="This &quot;such as&quot; is carrying too much weight. The size of a px is relative not to the font size, but to screen resolution and viewing distance. Furthermore, in CSS 3 the &quot;px&quot; becomes the primordial absolute unit as the length units become anchored to it. (March 2022)">clarification needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> – are <i id="mwAuQ">relative</i>, which means that factors such as the font size of a parent element can affect the rendered measurement. These eight units were a feature of CSS 1<sup about="#mwt210" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-15" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-15&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-15" id="mwAuU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuc">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAug">]</span></span></a></sup> and retained in all subsequent revisions. The proposed CSS Values and Units Module Level 3 will, if adopted as a W3C Recommendation, provide seven further length units: <code id="mwAuk">ch</code>; <code id="mwAuo">Q</code>; <code id="mwAus">rem</code>; <code id="mwAuw">vh</code>; <code id="mwAu0">vmax</code>; <code id="mwAu4">vmin</code>; and <code id="mwAu8">vw</code>.<sup about="#mwt213" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-16&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mwAvA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvI">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="12" id="mwAvQ" aria-labelledby="Use"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Use">Use</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Use"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAvU">Before CSS, nearly all presentational attributes of HTML documents were contained within the HTML markup. That is, all font colors, background styles, element alignments, borders, and sizes had to be explicitly described (often repeatedly) within the HTML. CSS lets authors move much of that information to another file, the style sheet, resulting in considerably simpler HTML. And additionally, as more and more devices are able to access responsive web pages, different screen sizes and layouts begin to appear. Customizing a website for each device size is costly and increasingly difficult. The modular nature of CSS means that styles can be reused in different parts of a site or even across sites, promoting consistency and efficiency.</p>

<p id="mwAvY">For example, headings (<code id="mwAvc">h1</code> elements), sub-headings (<code id="mwAvg">h2</code>), sub-sub-headings (<code id="mwAvk">h3</code>), etc., are defined structurally using HTML. In print and on the screen, choice of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Typeface" title="Typeface" id="mwAvo">font</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Point_(typography)" title="Point (typography)" id="mwAvs">size</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Color" title="Color" id="mwAvw">color</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emphasis_(typography)" title="Emphasis (typography)" id="mwAv0">emphasis</a> for these elements is <i id="mwAv4">presentational</i>.</p>

<p id="mwAv8">Before CSS, document authors who wanted to assign such <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Typography" title="Typography" id="mwAwA">typographic</a> characteristics to, say, all <code id="mwAwE">h2</code> headings had to repeat HTML presentational markup for each occurrence of that heading type. This made documents more complex, larger, and more error-prone and difficult to maintain. CSS allows the separation of presentation from structure. CSS can define color, font, text alignment, size, borders, spacing, layout and many other typographic characteristics, and can do so independently for on-screen and printed views. CSS also defines non-visual styles, such as reading speed and emphasis for aural text readers. The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/W3C" title="W3C" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAwI">W3C</a> has now <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deprecation" title="Deprecation" id="mwAwM">deprecated</a> the use of all presentational HTML markup.<sup about="#mwt216" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-17&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwAwQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAwU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwY">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAwg">For example, under pre-CSS HTML, a heading element defined with red text would be written as:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt217" id="mwAwk" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;h1&gt;&lt;font color=\&quot;red\&quot;&gt;Chapter 1.&lt;/font&gt;&lt;/h1&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwAwo"><span id="mwAws"></span><span class="p" id="mwAww">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwAw0">h1</span><span class="p" id="mwAw4">&gt;&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwAw8">font</span> <span class="na" id="mwAxA">color</span><span class="o" id="mwAxE">=</span><span class="s" id="mwAxI">"red"</span><span class="p" id="mwAxM">&gt;</span>Chapter 1.<span class="p" id="mwAxQ">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwAxU">font</span><span class="p" id="mwAxY">&gt;&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwAxc">h1</span><span class="p" id="mwAxg">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwAxk">Using CSS, the same element can be coded using style properties instead of HTML presentational attributes:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt218" id="mwAxo" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;h1 style=\&quot;color: red;\&quot;&gt;Chapter 1.&lt;/h1&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwAxs"><span id="mwAxw"></span><span class="p" id="mwAx0">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwAx4">h1</span> <span class="na" id="mwAx8">style</span><span class="o" id="mwAyA">=</span><span class="s" id="mwAyE">"color: red;"</span><span class="p" id="mwAyI">&gt;</span>Chapter 1.<span class="p" id="mwAyM">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwAyQ">h1</span><span class="p" id="mwAyU">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwAyY">The advantages of this may not be immediately clear but the power of CSS becomes more apparent when the style properties are placed in an internal style element or, even better, an external CSS file. For example, suppose the document contains the style element:</p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt219" id="mwAyc" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;style&gt;\n    h1 {\n        color: red;\n    }\n&lt;/style&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwAyg"><span id="mwAyk"></span><span class="p" id="mwAyo">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwAys">style</span><span class="p" id="mwAyw">&gt;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAy0">    </span><span class="nt" id="mwAy4">h1</span><span class="w" id="mwAy8"> </span><span class="p" id="mwAzA">{</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAzE">        </span><span class="k" id="mwAzI">color</span><span class="p" id="mwAzM">:</span><span class="w" id="mwAzQ"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwAzU">red</span><span class="p" id="mwAzY">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwAzc">    </span><span class="p" id="mwAzg">}</span>
<span class="p" id="mwAzk">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwAzo">style</span><span class="p" id="mwAzs">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwAzw">All <code id="mwAz0">h1</code> elements in the document will then automatically become red without requiring any explicit code. If the author later wanted to make <code id="mwAz4">h1</code> elements blue instead, this could be done by changing the style element to:</p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt220" id="mwAz8" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;style&gt;\n    h1 {\n        color: blue;\n    }\n&lt;/style&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwA0A"><span id="mwA0E"></span><span class="p" id="mwA0I">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwA0M">style</span><span class="p" id="mwA0Q">&gt;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA0U">    </span><span class="nt" id="mwA0Y">h1</span><span class="w" id="mwA0c"> </span><span class="p" id="mwA0g">{</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA0k">        </span><span class="k" id="mwA0o">color</span><span class="p" id="mwA0s">:</span><span class="w" id="mwA0w"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwA00">blue</span><span class="p" id="mwA04">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwA08">    </span><span class="p" id="mwA1A">}</span>
<span class="p" id="mwA1E">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwA1I">style</span><span class="p" id="mwA1M">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwA1Q">rather than by laboriously going through the document and changing the color for each individual <code id="mwA1U">h1</code> element.</p>

<p id="mwA1Y">The styles can also be placed in an external CSS file, as described below, and loaded using syntax similar to:</p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt221" id="mwA1c" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;link href=\&quot;path/to/file.css\&quot; rel=\&quot;stylesheet\&quot; type=\&quot;text/css\&quot;&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwA1g"><span id="mwA1k"></span><span class="p" id="mwA1o">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwA1s">link</span> <span class="na" id="mwA1w">href</span><span class="o" id="mwA10">=</span><span class="s" id="mwA14">"path/to/file.css"</span> <span class="na" id="mwA18">rel</span><span class="o" id="mwA2A">=</span><span class="s" id="mwA2E">"stylesheet"</span> <span class="na" id="mwA2I">type</span><span class="o" id="mwA2M">=</span><span class="s" id="mwA2Q">"text/css"</span><span class="p" id="mwA2U">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>

<p id="mwA2Y">This further decouples the styling from the HTML document and makes it possible to restyle multiple documents by simply editing a shared external CSS file.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwA2c" aria-labelledby="Sources"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Sources">Sources</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Sources"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA2g">CSS, or Cascading Style Sheets, offers a flexible way to style web content, with styles originating from browser defaults, user preferences, or web designers. These styles can be applied inline, within an HTML document, or through external .css files for broader consistency. Not only does this simplify web development by promoting reusability and maintainability, it also improves site performance because styles can be offloaded into dedicated .css files that browsers can cache. Additionally, even if the styles cannot be loaded or are disabled, this separation maintains the accessibility and readability of the content, ensuring that the site is usable for all users, including those with disabilities. Its multi-faceted approach, including considerations for selector specificity, rule order, and media types, ensures that websites are visually coherent and adaptive across different devices and user needs, striking a balance between design intent and user accessibility.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwA2k" aria-labelledby="Multiple_style_sheets"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Multiple_style_sheets">Multiple style sheets</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Multiple style sheets"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwA2o">Multiple style sheets can be imported. Different styles can be applied depending on the output device being used; for example, the screen version can be quite different from the printed version, so authors can tailor the presentation appropriately for each medium.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="15" id="mwA2s" aria-labelledby="Cascading"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Cascading">Cascading</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Cascading"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA2w">The style sheet with the highest priority controls the content display. Declarations not set in the highest priority source are passed on to a source of lower priority, such as the user agent style. The process is called <i id="mwA20">cascading</i>.</p>

<p id="mwA24">One of the goals of CSS is to allow users greater <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Style_sheet_(web_development)#Customization" title="Style sheet (web development)" id="mwA28">control over presentation</a>. Someone who finds red italic headings difficult to read may apply a different style sheet. Depending on the browser and the website, a user may choose from various style sheets provided by the designers, or may remove all added styles, and view the site using the browser's default styling, or may override just the red italic heading style without altering other attributes. Browser extensions like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stylish_(software)" title="Stylish (software)" id="mwA3A">Stylish</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stylus_(browser_extension)" title="Stylus (browser extension)" id="mwA3E">Stylus</a> have been created to facilitate the management of such user style sheets. In the case of large projects, cascading can be used to determine which style has a higher priority when developers do integrate third-party styles that have conflicting priorities, and to further resolve those conflicts. Additionally, cascading can help create themed designs, which help designers fine-tune aspects of a design without compromising the overall layout.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="16" id="mwA3I" aria-labelledby="CSS_priority_scheme"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="CSS_priority_scheme">CSS priority scheme</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: CSS priority scheme"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<table class="wikitable" id="mwA3M">
<caption id="mwA3Q">CSS priority scheme (highest to lowest)</caption>
<tbody id="mwA3U"><tr id="mwA3Y">
<th id="mwA3c">Priority</th><th id="mwA3g">CSS source type</th><th id="mwA3k">Description</th></tr>
<tr id="mwA3o">
<td id="mwA3s">1</td><td id="mwA3w">Importance</td><td id="mwA30">The "<code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt222" id="mwA34" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;!important&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;!important&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="o" id="mwA38">!</span><span class="nt" id="mwA4A">important</span></code>" annotation overwrites the previous priority types</td></tr>
<tr id="mwA4E">

<tr id="mwA4k">
<td id="mwA4o">4</td><td id="mwA4s">User defined</td><td id="mwA4w">Most browsers have the accessibility feature: a user-defined CSS</td></tr>

</tbody></table>

</section></section></section><section data-mw-section-id="17" id="mwA6Y" aria-labelledby="Specificity"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Specificity">Specificity</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Specificity"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA6c"><i id="mwA6g">Specificity</i> refers to the relative weights of various rules.<sup about="#mwt228" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Cascading_18-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Cascading&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-Cascading-18&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Cascading-18" id="mwA6k"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA6o"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6s">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6w">]</span></span></a></sup> It determines which styles apply to an element when more than one rule could apply.  Based on the specification, a simple selector (e.g. H1) has a specificity of 1, class selectors have a specificity of 1,0, and ID selectors have a specificity of 1,0,0. Because the specificity values do not carry over as in the decimal system, commas are used to separate the "digits"<sup about="#mwt231" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-19" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-19&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwA60"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA64"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA68">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7A">]</span></span></a></sup> (a CSS rule having 11 elements and 11 classes would have a specificity of 11,11, not 121).</p>

<p id="mwA7E">Thus the selectors of the following rule result in the indicated specificity:</p>
<table class="wikitable" id="mwA7I">
<tbody id="mwA7M"><tr id="mwA7Q">

<tr id="mwA7c">
<td id="mwA7g"><code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt232" id="mwA7k" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;h1 {color: white;}&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwA7o">h1</span><span class="w" id="mwA7s"> </span><span class="p" id="mwA7w">{</span><span class="k" id="mwA70">color</span><span class="p" id="mwA74">:</span><span class="w" id="mwA78"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwA8A">white</span><span class="p" id="mwA8E">;}</span></code></td><td id="mwA8I">0, 0, 0, 1</td></tr>

<section data-mw-section-id="18" id="mwBBI" aria-labelledby="Examples"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Examples">Examples</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Examples"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwBBM">Consider this HTML fragment:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt239" id="mwBBQ" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;!DOCTYPE html&gt;\n&lt;html&gt;\n    &lt;head&gt;\n        &lt;meta charset=\&quot;utf-8\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;style&gt;\n            #xyz { color: blue; }\n        &lt;/style&gt;\n    &lt;/head&gt;\n    &lt;body&gt;\n        &lt;p id=\&quot;xyz\&quot; style=\&quot;color: green;\&quot;&gt;To demonstrate specificity&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;/body&gt;\n&lt;/html&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBBU"><span id="mwBBY"></span><span class="cp" id="mwBBc">&lt;!DOCTYPE html&gt;</span>
<span class="p" id="mwBBg">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBBk">html</span><span class="p" id="mwBBo">&gt;</span>
    <span class="p" id="mwBBs">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBBw">head</span><span class="p" id="mwBB0">&gt;</span>
        <span class="p" id="mwBB4">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBB8">meta</span> <span class="na" id="mwBCA">charset</span><span class="o" id="mwBCE">=</span><span class="s" id="mwBCI">"utf-8"</span><span class="p" id="mwBCM">&gt;</span>
        <span class="p" id="mwBCQ">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBCU">style</span><span class="p" id="mwBCY">&gt;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBCc">            </span><span class="p" id="mwBCg">#</span><span class="nn" id="mwBCk">xyz</span><span class="w" id="mwBCo"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBCs">{</span><span class="w" id="mwBCw"> </span><span class="k" id="mwBC0">color</span><span class="p" id="mwBC4">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBC8"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBDA">blue</span><span class="p" id="mwBDE">;</span><span class="w" id="mwBDI"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBDM">}</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBDQ">        </span><span class="p" id="mwBDU">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBDY">style</span><span class="p" id="mwBDc">&gt;</span>
    <span class="p" id="mwBDg">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBDk">head</span><span class="p" id="mwBDo">&gt;</span>
    <span class="p" id="mwBDs">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBDw">body</span><span class="p" id="mwBD0">&gt;</span>
        <span class="p" id="mwBD4">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBD8">p</span> <span class="na" id="mwBEA">id</span><span class="o" id="mwBEE">=</span><span class="s" id="mwBEI">"xyz"</span> <span class="na" id="mwBEM">style</span><span class="o" id="mwBEQ">=</span><span class="s" id="mwBEU">"color: green;"</span><span class="p" id="mwBEY">&gt;</span>To demonstrate specificity<span class="p" id="mwBEc">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBEg">p</span><span class="p" id="mwBEk">&gt;</span>
    <span class="p" id="mwBEo">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBEs">body</span><span class="p" id="mwBEw">&gt;</span>
<span class="p" id="mwBE0">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBE4">html</span><span class="p" id="mwBE8">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>
<p id="mwBFA">In the above example, the declaration in the <code id="mwBFE">style</code> attribute overrides the one in the <code id="mwBFI">&lt;style&gt;</code> element because it has a higher specificity, and thus, the paragraph appears green:</p>

<div class="html-box" style="min-width: 160pt; height: 80pt; background-color: white; border: 2pt solid #303030; padding: 5px; user-select: none;" id="mwBFM">
     <p id="xyz" style="color: green;">To demonstrate specificity</p>

</div>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="19" id="mwBFQ" aria-labelledby="Inheritance"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Inheritance">Inheritance</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Inheritance"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBFU">Inheritance is a key feature in CSS; it relies on the ancestor-descendant relationship to operate. Inheritance is the mechanism by which properties are applied not only to a specified element but also to its descendants.<sup about="#mwt240" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Cascading_18-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Cascading&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Cascading-18" id="mwBFY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBFc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFg">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFk">]</span></span></a></sup> Inheritance relies on the document tree, which is the hierarchy of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XHTML" title="XHTML" id="mwBFo">XHTML</a> elements in a page based on nesting. Descendant elements may inherit CSS property values from any ancestor element enclosing them.
In general, descendant elements inherit text-related properties, but their box-related properties are not inherited. Properties that can be inherited are color, font, letter spacing, line-height, list-style, text-align, text-indent, text-transform, visibility, white-space, and word-spacing. Properties that cannot be inherited are background, border, display, float and clear, height, and width, margin, min- and max-height and -width, outline, overflow, padding, position, text-decoration, vertical-align, and z-index.</p>

<p id="mwBFs">Inheritance can be used to avoid declaring certain properties over and over again in a style sheet, allowing for shorter CSS.</p>

<p id="mwBFw">Inheritance in CSS is not the same as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Class-based_programming#Inheritance" title="Class-based programming" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBF0">inheritance in class-based programming languages</a>, where it is possible to define class B as "like class A, but with modifications".<sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-20" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-20&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-20" id="mwBF4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBF8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBGA">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBGE">]</span></span></a></sup> With CSS, it is possible to style an <i id="mwBGI">element</i> with "class A, but with modifications". However, it is not possible to define a CSS <i id="mwBGM">class</i> B like that, which could then be used to style multiple elements without having to repeat the modifications.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="20" id="mwBGQ" aria-labelledby="Example"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Example">Example</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Example"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBGU">Given the following style sheet:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt244" id="mwBGY" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\np {\n   color: blue;\n}\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBGc"><span id="mwBGg"></span><span class="nt" id="mwBGk">p</span><span class="w" id="mwBGo"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBGs">{</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBGw">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBG0">color</span><span class="p" id="mwBG4">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBG8"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBHA">blue</span><span class="p" id="mwBHE">;</span>
<span class="p" id="mwBHI">}</span>
</pre></div>
<p id="mwBHM">Suppose there is a p element with an emphasizing element (<code class="nowrap" style="" about="#mwt245" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBHQ" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Tag&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Tag&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;em&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;o&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span typeof="mw:Entity">&lt;</span>em<span typeof="mw:Entity">&gt;</span></code>) inside:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-html mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt246" id="mwBHU" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;html&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n&lt;p&gt;\n   This is to &lt;em&gt;illustrate&lt;/em&gt; inheritance\n&lt;/p&gt;\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBHY"><span id="mwBHc"></span><span class="p" id="mwBHg">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBHk">p</span><span class="p" id="mwBHo">&gt;</span>
   This is to <span class="p" id="mwBHs">&lt;</span><span class="nt" id="mwBHw">em</span><span class="p" id="mwBH0">&gt;</span>illustrate<span class="p" id="mwBH4">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBH8">em</span><span class="p" id="mwBIA">&gt;</span> inheritance
<span class="p" id="mwBIE">&lt;/</span><span class="nt" id="mwBII">p</span><span class="p" id="mwBIM">&gt;</span>
</pre></div>
<p id="mwBIQ">If no color is assigned to the em element, the emphasized word "illustrate" inherits the color of the parent element, p. The style sheet p has the color blue, hence, the em element is likewise blue:</p>

<div class="html-box" style="min-width: 160pt; height: 80pt; background-color: white; border: 2pt solid #303030; padding: 5px; user-select: none;" id="mwBIU">
  <p style="color: blue;" id="mwBIY">This is to <em id="mwBIc">illustrate</em> inheritance</p>

</div>
