</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="21" id="mwBIg" aria-labelledby="Whitespace"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Whitespace">Whitespace</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: Whitespace"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBIk">The whitespace between properties and selectors is ignored. This code snippet:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt247" id="mwBIo" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\nbody{overflow:hidden;background:#000000;background-image:url(images/bg.gif);background-repeat:no-repeat;background-position:left top;}\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBIs"><span id="mwBIw"></span><span class="nt" id="mwBI0">body</span><span class="p" id="mwBI4">{</span><span class="k" id="mwBI8">overflow</span><span class="p" id="mwBJA">:</span><span class="kc" id="mwBJE">hidden</span><span class="p" id="mwBJI">;</span><span class="k" id="mwBJM">background</span><span class="p" id="mwBJQ">:</span><span class="mh" id="mwBJU">#000000</span><span class="p" id="mwBJY">;</span><span class="k" id="mwBJc">background-image</span><span class="p" id="mwBJg">:</span><span class="nb" id="mwBJk">url</span><span class="p" id="mwBJo">(</span><span class="sx" id="mwBJs">images/bg.gif</span><span class="p" id="mwBJw">);</span><span class="k" id="mwBJ0">background-repeat</span><span class="p" id="mwBJ4">:</span><span class="kc" id="mwBJ8">no-repeat</span><span class="p" id="mwBKA">;</span><span class="k" id="mwBKE">background-position</span><span class="p" id="mwBKI">:</span><span class="kc" id="mwBKM">left</span><span class="w" id="mwBKQ"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBKU">top</span><span class="p" id="mwBKY">;}</span>
</pre></div>
<p id="mwBKc">is functionally equivalent to this one:</p>
<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt248" id="mwBKg" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\nbody {\n   overflow: hidden;\n   background-color: #000000;\n   background-image: url(images/bg.gif);\n   background-repeat: no-repeat;\n   background-position: left top;\n}\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBKk"><span id="mwBKo"></span><span class="nt" id="mwBKs">body</span><span class="w" id="mwBKw"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBK0">{</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBK4">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBK8">overflow</span><span class="p" id="mwBLA">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBLE"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBLI">hidden</span><span class="p" id="mwBLM">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBLQ">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBLU">background-color</span><span class="p" id="mwBLY">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBLc"> </span><span class="mh" id="mwBLg">#000000</span><span class="p" id="mwBLk">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBLo">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBLs">background-image</span><span class="p" id="mwBLw">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBL0"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBL4">url</span><span class="p" id="mwBL8">(</span><span class="sx" id="mwBMA">images/bg.gif</span><span class="p" id="mwBME">);</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBMI">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBMM">background-repeat</span><span class="p" id="mwBMQ">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBMU"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBMY">no-repeat</span><span class="p" id="mwBMc">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBMg">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBMk">background-position</span><span class="p" id="mwBMo">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBMs"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBMw">left</span><span class="w" id="mwBM0"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBM4">top</span><span class="p" id="mwBM8">;</span>
<span class="p" id="mwBNA">}</span>
</pre></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="22" id="mwBNE" aria-labelledby="Indentation"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Indentation">Indentation</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=22" title="Edit section: Indentation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt249" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBNI" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Main&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Main&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Indentation style&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt250" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt249" id="mwBNM">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indentation_style" title="Indentation style">Indentation style</a></div>
<p id="mwBNQ">One common way to format CSS for readability is to indent each property and give it its own line. In addition to formatting CSS for readability, shorthand properties can be used to write out the code faster, which also gets processed more quickly when being rendered:<sup about="#mwt253" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Mozilla_Developers_21-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Mozilla Developers&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-Mozilla_Developers-21&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-Mozilla_Developers-21" id="mwBNU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBNY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBNc">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBNg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt254" id="mwBNk" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\nbody {\n   overflow: hidden;\n   background: #000 url(images/bg.gif) no-repeat left top;\n}\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBNo"><span id="mwBNs"></span><span class="nt" id="mwBNw">body</span><span class="w" id="mwBN0"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBN4">{</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBN8">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBOA">overflow</span><span class="p" id="mwBOE">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBOI"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBOM">hidden</span><span class="p" id="mwBOQ">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBOU">   </span><span class="k" id="mwBOY">background</span><span class="p" id="mwBOc">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBOg"> </span><span class="mh" id="mwBOk">#000</span><span class="w" id="mwBOo"> </span><span class="nb" id="mwBOs">url</span><span class="p" id="mwBOw">(</span><span class="sx" id="mwBO0">images/bg.gif</span><span class="p" id="mwBO4">)</span><span class="w" id="mwBO8"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBPA">no-repeat</span><span class="w" id="mwBPE"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBPI">left</span><span class="w" id="mwBPM"> </span><span class="kc" id="mwBPQ">top</span><span class="p" id="mwBPU">;</span>
<span class="p" id="mwBPY">}</span>
</pre></div><p id="mwBPc">Sometimes, multiple property values are indented onto their own line:</p><div class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt255" id="mwBPg" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;\n@font-face {\n   font-family: 'Comic Sans';\n   font-size: 20px;\n   src: url('first.example.com'),\n        url('second.example.com'),\n        url('third.example.com'),\n        url('fourth.example.com');\n}\n&quot;}}"><pre id="mwBPk"><span id="mwBPo"></span><span class="p" id="mwBPs">@</span><span class="k" id="mwBPw">font-face</span><span class="w" id="mwBP0"> </span><span class="p" id="mwBP4">{</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBP8">   </span><span class="nt" id="mwBQA">font-family</span><span class="o" id="mwBQE">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBQI"> </span><span class="s1" id="mwBQM">'Comic Sans'</span><span class="o" id="mwBQQ">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBQU">   </span><span class="nt" id="mwBQY">font-size</span><span class="o" id="mwBQc">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBQg"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwBQk">20px</span><span class="o" id="mwBQo">;</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBQs">   </span><span class="nt" id="mwBQw">src</span><span class="o" id="mwBQ0">:</span><span class="w" id="mwBQ4"> </span><span class="nt" id="mwBQ8">url</span><span class="o" id="mwBRA">(</span><span class="s1" id="mwBRE">'first.example.com'</span><span class="o" id="mwBRI">),</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBRM">        </span><span class="nt" id="mwBRQ">url</span><span class="o" id="mwBRU">(</span><span class="s1" id="mwBRY">'second.example.com'</span><span class="o" id="mwBRc">),</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBRg">        </span><span class="nt" id="mwBRk">url</span><span class="o" id="mwBRo">(</span><span class="s1" id="mwBRs">'third.example.com'</span><span class="o" id="mwBRw">),</span>
<span class="w" id="mwBR0">        </span><span class="nt" id="mwBR4">url</span><span class="o" id="mwBR8">(</span><span class="s1" id="mwBSA">'fourth.example.com'</span><span class="o" id="mwBSE">);</span>
<span class="p" id="mwBSI">}</span>
</pre></div>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="23" id="mwBSM" aria-labelledby="Positioning"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Positioning">Positioning</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Positioning"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBSQ">CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBSU">&nbsp;</span>2.1 defines three positioning schemes:</p>
<dl id="mwBSY"><dt id="mwBSc">Normal flow</dt><dd id="mwBSg"><i id="mwBSk">Inline</i> items are laid out in the same way as the letters in words in the text, one after the other across the available space until there is no more room, then starting a new line below. <i id="mwBSo">Block</i> items stack vertically, like paragraphs and like the items in a bulleted list. Normal flow also includes the relative positioning of block or inline items and run-in boxes.</dd>
<dt id="mwBSs">Floats</dt><dd id="mwBSw">A floated item is taken out of the normal flow and shifted to the left or right as far as possible in the space available. Other content then flows alongside the floated item.</dd>
<dt id="mwBS0">Absolute positioning</dt><dd id="mwBS4">An absolutely positioned item has no place in, and no effect on, the normal flow of other items. It occupies its assigned position in its container independently of other items.<sup about="#mwt258" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-W3C-positioning_22-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;W3C-positioning&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-W3C-positioning-22&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-W3C-positioning-22" id="mwBS8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBTA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBTE">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBTI">]</span></span></a></sup></dd></dl>

<section data-mw-section-id="24" id="mwBTM" aria-labelledby="Position_property"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Position_property">Position property</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=24" title="Edit section: Position property"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBTQ">There are five possible values of the <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt259" id="mwBTU" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;position&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBTY">position</span></code> property. If an item is positioned in any way other than <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt260" id="mwBTc" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;static&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBTg">static</span></code>, then the further properties <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt261" id="mwBTk" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;top&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBTo">top</span></code>, <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt262" id="mwBTs" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;bottom&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBTw">bottom</span></code>, <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt263" id="mwBT0" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;left&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBT4">left</span></code>, and <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt264" id="mwBT8" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;CSS&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;right&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBUA">right</span></code> are used to specify offsets and positions.The element having position static is not affected by the  <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt265" id="mwBUE" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;top&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBUI">top</span></code>, <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt266" id="mwBUM" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;bottom&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBUQ">bottom</span></code> , <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt267" id="mwBUU" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;left&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBUY">left</span></code> or  <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight" about="#mwt268" id="mwBUc" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;right&quot;}}"><span class="nt" id="mwBUg">right</span></code> properties.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="25" id="mwBUk" aria-labelledby="Static"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="Static">Static</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=25" title="Edit section: Static"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBUo">The default value places the item in the <i id="mwBUs">normal flow</i>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="26" id="mwBUw" aria-labelledby="Relative"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="Relative">Relative</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=26" title="Edit section: Relative"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBU0">The item is placed in the <i id="mwBU4">normal flow</i>, and then shifted or offset from that position. Subsequent flow items are laid out as if the item had not been moved.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="27" id="mwBU8" aria-labelledby="Absolute"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="Absolute">Absolute</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=27" title="Edit section: Absolute"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBVA">Specifies <i id="mwBVE">absolute positioning</i>. The element is positioned in relation to its nearest non-static ancestor.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="28" id="mwBVI" aria-labelledby="Fixed"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="Fixed">Fixed</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=28" title="Edit section: Fixed"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBVM">The item is <i id="mwBVQ">absolutely positioned</i> in a fixed position on the screen even as the rest of the document is scrolled<sup about="#mwt269" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-W3C-positioning_22-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;W3C-positioning&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-W3C-positioning-22" id="mwBVU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBVY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBVc">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBVg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="29" id="mwBVk" aria-labelledby="Float_and_clear"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Float_and_clear">Float and clear</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=29" title="Edit section: Float and clear"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBVo">The <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt270" id="mwBVs" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;float&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;float&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwBVw">float</span></code> property may have one of three values. <i id="mwBV0">Absolutely</i> positioned or <i id="mwBV4">fixed</i> items cannot be floated. Other elements normally flow around floated items, unless they are prevented from doing so by their <code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt272" id="mwBV8" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;clear&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;clear&quot;},&quot;2&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwBWA">clear</span></code> property.</p>

<dl id="mwBWE"><dt id="mwBWI">left</dt><dd id="mwBWM">The item <i id="mwBWQ">floats</i> to the left of the line that it would have appeared in; other items may flow around its right side.</dd>
<dt id="mwBWU">right</dt><dd id="mwBWY">The item <i id="mwBWc">floats</i> to the right of the line that it would have appeared in; other items may flow around its left side.</dd>
<dt id="mwBWg">clear</dt><dd id="mwBWk">Forces the element to appear underneath ('clear') floated elements to the left (<code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt274" id="mwBWo" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;clear:left&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;clear:left&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwBWs">clear</span><span class="p" id="mwBWw">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwBW0">left</span></code>), right (<code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt276" id="mwBW4" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;clear:right&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;clear:right&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwBW8">clear</span><span class="p" id="mwBXA">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwBXE">right</span></code>) or both sides (<code class="mw-highlight mw-highlight-lang-css mw-content-ltr" style="" dir="ltr" typeof="mw:Extension/syntaxhighlight mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt278" id="mwBXI" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;syntaxhighlight&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;css&quot;,&quot;class&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;style&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;inline&quot;:&quot;1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;clear:both&quot;},&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;code&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Code&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;css&quot;},&quot;code&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;clear:both&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><span class="nt" id="mwBXM">clear</span><span class="p" id="mwBXQ">:</span><span class="nd" id="mwBXU">both</span></code>).<sup about="#mwt280" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-W3C-positioning_22-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;W3C-positioning&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-W3C-positioning-22" id="mwBXY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBXc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBXg">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBXk">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt283" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-23&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwBXo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBXs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBXw">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBX0">]</span></span></a></sup></dd></dl>

</section></section></section><section data-mw-section-id="30" id="mwBX4" aria-labelledby="History"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="History">History</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=30" title="Edit section: History"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-right" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBX8"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Håkon_Wium_Lie.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBYA"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Håkon_Wium_Lie.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/54/H%C3%A5kon_Wium_Lie.jpg/250px-H%C3%A5kon_Wium_Lie.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/54/H%C3%A5kon_Wium_Lie.jpg/500px-H%C3%A5kon_Wium_Lie.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="1920" data-file-height="2560" data-file-type="bitmap" height="253" width="190" class="mw-file-element mw-file-upright" style="--mw-file-upright: 0.75" id="mwBYE"></a><figcaption id="mwBYI"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Håkon_Wium_Lie" title="Håkon Wium Lie" id="mwBYM">Håkon Wium Lie</a>, chief technical officer of the Opera Software company and co-creator of the CSS web standards</figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwBYQ">CSS was first proposed by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Håkon_Wium_Lie" title="Håkon Wium Lie" id="mwBYU">Håkon Wium Lie</a> on 10 October 1994.<sup about="#mwt286" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chss-proposal_24-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chss-proposal&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-chss-proposal-24&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chss-proposal-24" id="mwBYY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBYc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBYg">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBYk">]</span></span></a></sup> At the time, Lie was working with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tim_Berners-Lee" title="Tim Berners-Lee" id="mwBYo">Tim Berners-Lee</a> at <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CERN" title="CERN" id="mwBYs">CERN</a>.<sup about="#mwt289" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chapter20_25-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chapter20&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-chapter20-25&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chapter20-25" id="mwBYw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBY0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBY4">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBY8">]</span></span></a></sup> Several other style sheet languages for the web were proposed around the same time, and discussions on public mailing lists and inside <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Wide_Web_Consortium" title="World Wide Web Consortium" id="mwBZA">World Wide Web Consortium</a> resulted in the first W3C CSS Recommendation (CSS1)<sup about="#mwt292" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-w3c-css1_26-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;w3c-css1&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-w3c-css1-26&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-w3c-css1-26" id="mwBZE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBZI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBZM">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBZQ">]</span></span></a></sup> being released in 1996. In particular, a proposal by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bert_Bos" title="Bert Bos" id="mwBZU">Bert Bos</a> was influential; he became co-author of CSS1, and is regarded as co-creator of CSS.<sup about="#mwt295" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-WWW3_27-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;WWW3&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-WWW3-27&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-WWW3-27" id="mwBZY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBZc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBZg">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBZk">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBZo">Style sheets have existed in one form or another since the beginnings of Standard Generalized Markup Language (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SGML" title="SGML" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBZs">SGML</a>) in the 1980s, and CSS was developed to provide style sheets for the web.<sup about="#mwt298" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-css-phd_28-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;css-phd&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-css-phd-28&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-css-phd-28" id="mwBZw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBZ0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBZ4">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBZ8">]</span></span></a></sup> One requirement for a web style sheet language was for style sheets to come from different sources on the web. Therefore, existing style sheet languages like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Document_Style_Semantics_and_Specification_Language" title="Document Style Semantics and Specification Language" id="mwBaA">DSSSL</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Formatting_Output_Specification_Instance" title="Formatting Output Specification Instance" id="mwBaE">FOSI</a> were not suitable. CSS, on the other hand, let a document's style be influenced by multiple style sheets by way of "cascading" styles.<sup about="#mwt299" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-css-phd_28-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;css-phd&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-css-phd-28" id="mwBaI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBaM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBaQ">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBaU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBaY">As HTML grew, it came to encompass a wider variety of stylistic capabilities to meet the demands of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_development" title="Web development" id="mwBac">web developers</a>. This evolution gave the designer more control over site appearance, at the cost of more complex HTML. Variations in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_browser" title="Web browser" id="mwBag">web browser</a> implementations, such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ViolaWWW" title="ViolaWWW" id="mwBak">ViolaWWW</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WorldWideWeb" title="WorldWideWeb" id="mwBao">WorldWideWeb</a>,<sup about="#mwt302" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-IEEE_29-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;IEEE&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-IEEE-29&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-IEEE-29" id="mwBas"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBaw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBa0">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBa4">]</span></span></a></sup> made consistent site appearance difficult, and users had less control over how web content was displayed. The browser/editor developed by Tim Berners-Lee had style sheets that were hard-coded into the program. The style sheets could therefore not be linked to documents on the web.<sup about="#mwt303" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chapter20_25-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chapter20&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chapter20-25" id="mwBa8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBbA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBbE">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBbI">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Cailliau" title="Robert Cailliau" id="mwBbM">Robert Cailliau</a>, also of CERN, wanted to separate the structure from the presentation so that different style sheets could describe different presentation for printing, screen-based presentations, and editors.<sup about="#mwt304" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-IEEE_29-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;IEEE&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-IEEE-29" id="mwBbQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBbU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBbY">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBbc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBbg">Improving web presentation capabilities was a topic of interest to many in the web community and nine different style sheet languages were proposed on the www-style mailing list.<sup about="#mwt305" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-css-phd_28-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;css-phd&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-css-phd-28" id="mwBbk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBbo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBbs">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBbw">]</span></span></a></sup> Of these nine proposals, two were especially influential on what became CSS: Cascading HTML Style Sheets<sup about="#mwt306" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chss-proposal_24-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chss-proposal&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chss-proposal-24" id="mwBb0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBb4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBb8">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBcA">]</span></span></a></sup> and Stream-based Style Sheet Proposal (SSP).<sup about="#mwt307" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-WWW3_27-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;WWW3&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-WWW3-27" id="mwBcE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBcI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBcM">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBcQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt310" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ssp_30-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ssp&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-ssp-30&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-ssp-30" id="mwBcU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBcY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBcc">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBcg">]</span></span></a></sup> Two browsers served as testbeds for the initial proposals; Lie worked with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yves_Lafon?action=edit&amp;redlink=1" title="Yves Lafon (page does not exist)" class="new" typeof="mw:LocalizedAttrs" id="mwBck" data-mw-i18n="{&quot;title&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;x-page&quot;,&quot;key&quot;:&quot;red-link-title&quot;,&quot;params&quot;:[&quot;Yves Lafon&quot;]}}">Yves Lafon</a> to implement CSS in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dave_Raggett" title="Dave Raggett" id="mwBco">Dave Raggett</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arena_(web_browser)" title="Arena (web browser)" id="mwBcs">Arena</a> browser.<sup about="#mwt313" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-31" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-31&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-31" id="mwBcw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBc0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBc4">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBc8">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt316" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-32" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-32&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mwBdA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBdE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBdI">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBdM">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt319" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-33" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-33&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-33" id="mwBdQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBdU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBdY">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBdc">]</span></span></a></sup> Bert Bos implemented his own SSP proposal in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Argo_(web_browser)" title="Argo (web browser)" id="mwBdg">Argo</a> browser.<sup about="#mwt320" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-WWW3_27-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;WWW3&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-WWW3-27" id="mwBdk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBdo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBds">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBdw">]</span></span></a></sup> Thereafter, Lie and Bos worked together to develop the CSS standard (the 'H' was removed from the name because these style sheets could also be applied to other markup languages besides HTML).<sup about="#mwt321" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chapter20_25-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chapter20&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chapter20-25" id="mwBd0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBd4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBd8">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBeA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBeE">Lie's proposal was presented at the "<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mosaic_and_the_Web?action=edit&amp;redlink=1" title="Mosaic and the Web (page does not exist)" class="new" typeof="mw:LocalizedAttrs" id="mwBeI" data-mw-i18n="{&quot;title&quot;:{&quot;lang&quot;:&quot;x-page&quot;,&quot;key&quot;:&quot;red-link-title&quot;,&quot;params&quot;:[&quot;Mosaic and the Web&quot;]}}">Mosaic and the Web</a>" conference (later called WWW2) in Chicago, Illinois in 1994, and again with Bert Bos in 1995.<sup about="#mwt322" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chapter20_25-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chapter20&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chapter20-25" id="mwBeM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBeQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBeU">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBeY">]</span></span></a></sup> Around this time the W3C was already being established and took an interest in the development of CSS. It organized a workshop toward that end chaired by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steven_Pemberton" title="Steven Pemberton" id="mwBec">Steven Pemberton</a>. This resulted in W3C adding work on CSS to the deliverables of the HTML editorial review board (ERB). Lie and Bos were the primary technical staff on this aspect of the project, with additional members, including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Reardon" title="Thomas Reardon" id="mwBeg">Thomas Reardon</a> of Microsoft, participating as well. In August 1996, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Netscape" title="Netscape" id="mwBek">Netscape</a> Communication Corporation presented an alternative style sheet language called <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/JavaScript_Style_Sheets" title="JavaScript Style Sheets" id="mwBeo">JavaScript Style Sheets</a> (JSSS).<sup about="#mwt323" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chapter20_25-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chapter20&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chapter20-25" id="mwBes"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBew"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBe0">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBe4">]</span></span></a></sup> The spec was never finished, and is deprecated.<sup about="#mwt326" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-34" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-34&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-34" id="mwBe8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBfA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBfE">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBfI">]</span></span></a></sup> By the end of 1996, CSS was ready to become official, and the CSS level 1 Recommendation was published in December.</p>

<p id="mwBfM">Development of HTML, CSS, and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Document_Object_Model" title="Document Object Model" id="mwBfQ">DOM</a> had all been taking place in one group, the HTML Editorial Review Board (ERB). Early in 1997, the ERB was split into three working groups: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML_Working_Group" title="HTML Working Group" id="mwBfU">HTML Working Group</a>, chaired by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dan_Connolly_(computer_scientist)" title="Dan Connolly (computer scientist)" id="mwBfY">Dan Connolly</a> of W3C; DOM Working group, chaired by Lauren Wood of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SoftQuad" title="SoftQuad" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBfc">SoftQuad</a>; and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CSS_Working_Group" title="CSS Working Group" id="mwBfg">CSS Working Group</a>, chaired by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chris_Lilley_(W3C)" title="Chris Lilley (W3C)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBfk">Chris Lilley</a> of W3C.</p>

<p id="mwBfo">The CSS Working Group began tackling issues that had not been addressed with CSS level 1, resulting in the creation of CSS level 2 on November 4, 1997. It was published as a W3C Recommendation on May 12, 1998. CSS level 3, which was started in 1998, is still under development <span about="#mwt327" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBfs" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;as of&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:As_of&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;2014&quot;},&quot;lc&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;y&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">as of 2014</span><sup class="plainlinks noexcerpt noprint asof-tag update" style="display:none;" about="#mwt327"><a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span>update<span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></a></sup><span typeof="mw:Nowiki" about="#mwt327"></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_potentially_dated_statements_from_2014" about="#mwt327"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_containing_potentially_dated_statements" about="#mwt327" id="mwBfw">.</p>

<p id="mwBf0">In 2005, the CSS Working Groups decided to enforce the requirements for standards more strictly. This meant that already published standards like CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBf4">&nbsp;</span>2.1, CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBf8">&nbsp;</span>3 Selectors, and CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBgA">&nbsp;</span>3 Text were pulled back from Candidate Recommendation to Working Draft level.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="31" id="mwBgE" aria-labelledby="Difficulty_with_adoption"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Difficulty_with_adoption">Difficulty with adoption</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=31" title="Edit section: Difficulty with adoption"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBgI">The CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBgM">&nbsp;</span>1 specification was completed in 1996. Microsoft's <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_3" title="Internet Explorer 3" id="mwBgQ">Internet Explorer 3</a><sup about="#mwt328" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-chapter20_25-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;chapter20&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-chapter20-25" id="mwBgU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBgY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBgc">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBgg">]</span></span></a></sup> was released that year, featuring some limited support for CSS.  <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_4" title="Internet Explorer 4" id="mwBgk">IE 4</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Netscape_Navigator" title="Netscape Navigator" id="mwBgo">Netscape 4.x</a> added more support, but it was typically incomplete and had many <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Software_bug" title="Software bug" id="mwBgs">bugs</a> that prevented CSS from being usefully adopted. It was more than three years before any web browser achieved near-full implementation of the specification. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_for_Mac" title="Internet Explorer for Mac" id="mwBgw">Internet Explorer 5.0</a> for the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_Macintosh" title="Apple Macintosh" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBg0">Macintosh</a>, shipped in March 2000, was the first browser to have full (better than 99 percent) CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBg4">&nbsp;</span>1 support,<sup about="#mwt331" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-35" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-35&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-35" id="mwBg8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBhA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBhE">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBhI">]</span></span></a></sup> surpassing <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opera_(web_browser)" title="Opera (web browser)" id="mwBhM">Opera</a>, which had been the leader since its introduction of CSS support fifteen months earlier. Other browsers followed soon afterward, and many of them additionally implemented parts of CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBhQ">&nbsp;</span>2.</p>

<p id="mwBhU">However, even when later "version 5" web browsers began to offer a fairly full implementation of CSS, they were still incorrect in certain areas. They were fraught with inconsistencies, bugs, and other <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quirks_mode" title="Quirks mode" id="mwBhY">quirks</a>. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_5" title="Internet Explorer 5" id="mwBhc">Microsoft Internet Explorer 5. x for Windows</a>, as opposed to the very different <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_for_Mac" title="Internet Explorer for Mac" id="mwBhg">IE for Macintosh</a>, had a flawed implementation of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CSS_box_model" title="CSS box model" id="mwBhk">CSS box model</a>, as compared with the CSS standards. Such inconsistencies and variation in feature support made it difficult for designers to achieve a consistent appearance across browsers and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Computing_platform" title="Computing platform" id="mwBho">platforms</a> without the use of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Workaround" title="Workaround" id="mwBhs">workarounds</a> termed <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CSS_hack" title="CSS hack" id="mwBhw">CSS hacks</a> and filters. The IE Windows box model bugs were so serious that, when <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_6" title="Internet Explorer 6" id="mwBh0">Internet Explorer 6</a> was released, Microsoft introduced a backward-compatible mode of CSS interpretation ("<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quirks_mode" title="Quirks mode" id="mwBh4">quirks mode</a>") alongside an alternative, corrected "standards mode". Other non-Microsoft browsers also provided mode-switch capabilities. It, therefore, became necessary for authors of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTML" title="HTML" id="mwBh8">HTML</a> files to ensure they contained special distinctive <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Document_type_declaration#HTML5_DTD-less_DOCTYPE" title="Document type declaration" id="mwBiA">"standards-compliant CSS intended" marker</a> to show that the authors intended CSS to be interpreted correctly, in compliance with standards, as opposed to being intended for the now long-obsolete <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_Explorer_5" title="Internet Explorer 5" id="mwBiE">IE5/Windows browser</a>. Without this marker, web browsers with the "quirks mode"-switching capability will size objects in web pages as IE 5 on Windows would, rather than following CSS standards.</p>

<p id="mwBiI">Problems with the patchy adoption of CSS and errata in the original specification led the W3C to revise the CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBiM">&nbsp;</span>2 standards into CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBiQ">&nbsp;</span>2.1, which moved nearer to a working snapshot of current CSS support in HTML browsers. Some CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBiU">&nbsp;</span>2 properties that no browser successfully implemented were dropped, and in a few cases, defined behaviors were changed to bring the standard into line with the predominant existing implementations. CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBiY">&nbsp;</span>2.1 became a Candidate Recommendation on February 25, 2004, but CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBic">&nbsp;</span>2.1 was pulled back to Working Draft status on June 13, 2005,<sup about="#mwt334" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-36" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-36&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-36" id="mwBig"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBik"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBio">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBis">]</span></span></a></sup> and only returned to Candidate Recommendation status on July 19, 2007.<sup about="#mwt337" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-37" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-37&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-37" id="mwBiw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBi0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBi4">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBi8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBjA">In addition to these problems, the <code id="mwBjE">.css</code> extension was used by a software product used to convert <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microsoft_PowerPoint" title="Microsoft PowerPoint" id="mwBjI">PowerPoint</a> files into Compact Slide Show files,<sup about="#mwt340" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-38" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-38&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-38" id="mwBjM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBjQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBjU">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBjY">]</span></span></a></sup>
so some web servers served all <code id="mwBjc">.css</code><sup about="#mwt343" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-39" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-39&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-39" id="mwBjg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBjk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBjo">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBjs">]</span></span></a></sup> as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_media_type" title="Internet media type" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBjw">MIME type</a> <code id="mwBj0">application/x-pointplus</code><sup about="#mwt346" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-40" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-40&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-40" id="mwBj4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBj8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBkA">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBkE">]</span></span></a></sup> rather than <code id="mwBkI">text/css</code>.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="32" id="mwBkM" aria-labelledby="Vendor_prefixes"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Vendor_prefixes">Vendor prefixes</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=32" title="Edit section: Vendor prefixes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBkQ">Individual browser vendors occasionally introduced new parameters ahead of standardization and universalization. To prevent interfering with future implementations, vendors prepended unique names to the parameters, such as <code id="mwBkU">-moz-</code> for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mozilla_Firefox" title="Mozilla Firefox" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBkY">Mozilla Firefox</a>, <code id="mwBkc">-webkit-</code> named after <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WebKit" title="WebKit" id="mwBkg">the browsing engine</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_Safari" title="Apple Safari" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBkk">Apple Safari</a>, <code id="mwBko">-o-</code> for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opera_Browser" title="Opera Browser" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBks">Opera Browser</a> and <code id="mwBkw">-ms-</code> for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microsoft_Internet_Explorer" title="Microsoft Internet Explorer" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBk0">Microsoft Internet Explorer</a> and early versions of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microsoft_Edge" title="Microsoft Edge" id="mwBk4">Microsoft Edge</a> that use EdgeHTML.</p>

<p id="mwBk8">Occasionally, the parameters with vendor prefixes such as <code id="mwBlA">-moz-radial-gradient</code> and <code id="mwBlE">-webkit-linear-gradient</code> have slightly different syntax as compared to their non-vendor-prefix counterparts.<sup about="#mwt349" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-41" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-41&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-41" id="mwBlI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBlM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBlQ">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBlU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBlY">Prefixed properties are rendered obsolete by the time of standardization. Programs are available to automatically add prefixes for older browsers and to point out standardized versions of prefixed parameters. Since prefixes are limited to a small subset of browsers, removing the prefix allows other browsers to see the functionality. An exception is certain obsolete <code id="mwBlc">-webkit-</code> prefixed properties, which are so common and persistent on the web that other families of browsers have decided to support them for compatibility.<sup about="#mwt352" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-42" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-42&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-42" id="mwBlg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBlk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBlo">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBls">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBlw"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:W3C_CSS_Snapshot.png" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBl0"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:W3C_CSS_Snapshot.png" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b5/W3C_CSS_Snapshot.png/250px-W3C_CSS_Snapshot.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b5/W3C_CSS_Snapshot.png/500px-W3C_CSS_Snapshot.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="794" data-file-height="1123" data-file-type="bitmap" height="354" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwBl4"></a><figcaption id="mwBl8">CSS Snapshot 2021</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwBmA">CSS has various levels and profiles. Each level of CSS builds upon the last, typically adding new features and typically denoted<sup about="#mwt355" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-43" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-43&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-43" id="mwBmE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBmI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBmM">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBmQ">]</span></span></a></sup>  as CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBmU">&nbsp;</span>1, CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBmY">&nbsp;</span>2, CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBmc">&nbsp;</span>3, and CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBmg">&nbsp;</span>4. Profiles are typically a subset of one or more levels of CSS built for a particular device or user interface. Currently, there are profiles for mobile devices, printers, and television sets. Profiles should not be confused with media types, which were added in CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBmk">&nbsp;</span>2.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="33" id="mwBmo" aria-labelledby="CSS_1"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="CSS_1">CSS 1</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=33" title="Edit section: CSS 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBms">The first  CSS specification to become an official W3C Recommendation is CSS level 1, published on 17 December 1996.  <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Håkon_Wium_Lie" title="Håkon Wium Lie" id="mwBmw">Håkon Wium Lie</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bert_Bos" title="Bert Bos" id="mwBm0">Bert Bos</a> are credited as the original developers.<sup about="#mwt358" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-44" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-44&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-44" id="mwBm4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBm8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBnA">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBnE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt360" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-45" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-45&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-45" id="mwBnI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBnM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBnQ">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBnU">]</span></span></a></sup> Among its capabilities are support for</p>

<ul id="mwBnY"><li id="mwBnc"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Typeface" title="Typeface" id="mwBng">Font</a> properties such as typeface and emphasis</li>
<li id="mwBnk">Color of text, backgrounds, and other elements</li>
<li id="mwBno">Text attributes such as spacing between words, letters, and lines of text</li>
<li id="mwBns"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alignment_(typesetting)" title="Alignment (typesetting)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBnw">Alignment</a> of text, images, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Table_(HTML)" title="Table (HTML)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBn0">tables</a> and other elements</li>
<li id="mwBn4">Margin, border, padding, and positioning for most elements</li>
<li id="mwBn8">Unique identification and generic classification of groups of attributes</li></ul>

<p id="mwBoA">The W3C no longer maintains the CSS 1 Recommendation.<sup about="#mwt362" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-46" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-46&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-46" id="mwBoE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBoI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBoM">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBoQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="34" id="mwBoU" aria-labelledby="CSS_2"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="CSS_2">CSS 2</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=34" title="Edit section: CSS 2"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBoY">CSS level 2 specification was developed by the W3C and published as a recommendation in May 1998. A superset of CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBoc">&nbsp;</span>1, CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBog">&nbsp;</span>2 includes a number of new capabilities like absolute, relative, and fixed positioning of elements and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Z-index" title="Z-index" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBok">z-index</a>, the concept of media types, support for aural style sheets (which were later replaced by the CSS 3 speech modules)<sup about="#mwt365" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-47" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-47&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-47" id="mwBoo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBos"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBow">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBo0">]</span></span></a></sup> and bidirectional text, and new font properties such as shadows.</p>

<p id="mwBo4">The W3C no longer maintains the CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBo8">&nbsp;</span>2 recommendation.<sup about="#mwt367" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-48" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-48&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-48" id="mwBpA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBpE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBpI">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBpM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="35" id="mwBpQ" aria-labelledby="CSS_2.1"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="CSS_2.1">CSS 2.1</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=35" title="Edit section: CSS 2.1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBpU">CSS level 2 revision 1, often referred to as "CSS 2.1", fixes errors in CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBpY">&nbsp;</span>2, removes poorly supported or not fully interoperable features and adds already implemented browser extensions to the specification. To comply with the W3C Process for standardizing technical specifications, CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBpc">&nbsp;</span>2.1 went back and forth between Working Draft status and Candidate Recommendation status for many years. CSS 2.1 first became a <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.w3.org/TR/2004/CR-CSS21-20040225/" class="external text" id="mwBpg">Candidate Recommendation</a> on 25 February 2004, but it was reverted to a Working Draft on 13 June 2005 for further review. It returned to Candidate Recommendation on 19 July 2007 and then updated twice in 2009. However, because changes and clarifications were made, it again went back to Last Call Working Draft on 7 December 2010.</p>

<p id="mwBpk">CSS 2.1 went to Proposed Recommendation on 12 April 2011.<sup about="#mwt369" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-49" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-49&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-49" id="mwBpo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBps"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBpw">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBp0">]</span></span></a></sup> After being reviewed by the W3C Advisory Committee, it was finally published as a W3C Recommendation on 7 June 2011.<sup about="#mwt371" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-w3.org_50-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;w3.org&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-w3.org-50&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-w3.org-50" id="mwBp4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBp8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBqA">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBqE">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBqI">CSS 2.1 was planned as the first and final revision of level 2—but low-priority work on CSS 2.2 began in 2015.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="36" id="mwBqM" aria-labelledby="CSS_3"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="CSS_3">CSS 3</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=CSS&amp;action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: CSS 3"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt372" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBqQ" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;redirect&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Redirect&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;1&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;CSS3&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt373" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt372" id="mwBqU">"CSS3" redirects here. For other uses, see <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CSS3_(disambiguation)" title="CSS3 (disambiguation)" class="mw-disambig">CSS3 (disambiguation)</a>.</div>

<p id="mwBqY">Unlike CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBqc">&nbsp;</span>2, which is a large single specification defining various features, CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBqg">&nbsp;</span>3 is divided into several separate documents called "modules". Each module adds new capabilities or extends features defined in CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBqk">&nbsp;</span>2, preserving backward compatibility. Work on CSS level 3 started around the time of publication of the original CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBqo">&nbsp;</span>2 recommendation. The earliest CSS<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwBqs">&nbsp;</span>3 drafts were published in June 1999.<sup about="#mwt376" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-World_Wide_Web_Consortium_51-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;World Wide Web Consortium&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-World_Wide_Web_Consortium-51&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-World_Wide_Web_Consortium-51" id="mwBqw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBq0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBq4">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBq8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBrA">Due to the modularization, different modules have different stability and statuses.<sup about="#mwt379" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-52" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-52&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-52" id="mwBrE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBrI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBrM">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBrQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBrU">Some modules have <i id="mwBrY"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/W3C_Recommendation#Candidate_recommendation_(CR)" title="W3C Recommendation" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBrc">Candidate Recommendation</a></i> (<i id="mwBrg">CR</i>) status and are considered moderately stable. At <i id="mwBrk">CR</i> stage, implementations are advised to drop vendor prefixes.<sup about="#mwt382" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-53" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ref&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;mw-reference-text-cite_note-53&quot;}}"><a href="#cite_note-53" id="mwBro"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBrs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBrw">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBr0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
